THESE WRIT PETITIONS ARE FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226
AND 227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO
DECLARE THAT SECTION 20(2)(a-iv) AND (a-v) OF THE
KARNATAKA CO-OPERATIVE SOCIEITIES ACT, 1959 ARE ULTRA
VIRES OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA, ISSUE A WRIT OF
CERTIORARI TO QUASH THE DISQUALIFICATION VOTER LIST
PASSED BY THE RESPONDENT NO.4 VIDE ANNEXURE-B SOFAR
CONCERNT TO THE PETITIONERS AND ISSUE A WRIT OF
MANDAMUS DIRECTING THE 2, 3 AND 4TH RESPONDENTS TO
INCLUDE THE PETITIONERS IN THE LIST OF VALID VOTERS
WHILE PUBLISHING THE FINAL VALID VOTERS LIST.
 THESE WRIT PETITIONS COMING ON FOR PRELIMINARY
HEARING THIS DAY, THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:

206
ORDER
Learned counsel for the petitioners files a memo
seeking leave to withdraw the writ petitions.
 Memo is placed on record. The writ petitions are
dismissed as withdrawn.
